        function matchFunding() {
            const amount = parseInt(document.getElementById('fundingAmount').value) || 0;
            const stage = document.getElementById('businessStage').value;
            const credit = document.getElementById('creditScore').value;
            const type = document.getElementById('fundingType').value;
            const location = document.getElementById('location').value.toUpperCase();

            let matches = fundingOptions.filter(option => {
                const amtMatch = amount >= (option.amount.includes('Up') ? 5000 : parseInt(option.amount.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')));
                const stageMatch = option.stage.includes(stage);
                const creditMatch = option.credit.includes(credit) || option.credit === 'All' || option.credit === 'N/A';
                const typeMatch = !type || option.type === type || (type === 'loan' && option.type === 'loan');
                const stateMatch = !location || option.state === 'All' || option.state.includes(location);
                return amtMatch && stageMatch && creditMatch && typeMatch && stateMatch;
            });

            if (type === 'loan') matches = matches.filter(m => m.type === 'loan' || m.name.includes('SBA'));
            if (type === 'grant') matches = matches.filter(m => m.type === 'grant');

            matches.sort((a, b) => (a.type === type ? -1 : 1));
            matches = matches.slice(0, 6);

            const list = document.getElementById('matchList');
            if (matches.length === 0) {
                list.innerHTML = '<p style="text-align: center; font-style: italic;">No exact matches—broaden your criteria for more options!</p>';
            } else {
                list.innerHTML = matches.map(match => `
                    <div class="match-card">
                        <h4>${match.name}</h4>
                        <p>${match.desc}</p>
                        <p><strong>Amount:</strong> ${match.amount} | <strong>Eligibility:</strong> ${match.eligibility}</p>
                        <a href="${match.link}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Apply Now</a>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
            }

            document.getElementById('results').style.display = 'block';
            window.scrollTo({ top: document.getElementById('results').offsetTop - 100, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }

        function applyNow() {
            const amount = document.getElementById('fundingAmount').value;
            const stage = document.getElementById('businessStage').options[document.getElementById('businessStage').selectedIndex].text;
            const matches = document.querySelectorAll('.match-card');
            if (matches.length > 0) {
                const topLink = matches[0].querySelector('a').href;
                alert(`Redirecting to top match for $${amount} ${stage.toLowerCase()}...\n\nPre-filled details ready.`);
                window.open(topLink, '_blank');
            } else {
                alert('Run a match first!');
            }
        }

        // Auto-match on change
        document.querySelectorAll('input, select').forEach(el => {
            el.addEventListener('change', matchFunding);
        });

        // Initial load with default
        window.onload = () => matchFunding();
    </script>
</body>
</html>

    <script>
        // Chart Instances
        let tpdChart, revenueChart, profitChart, occupancyChart;

        // Real-time occupancy display
        document.getElementById('occupancyRate').addEventListener('input', function() {
            document.getElementById('occupancyValue').textContent = this.value + '%';
            calculateTPD();
        });

        // Auto-calculate on any input change
        document.querySelectorAll('input, select').forEach(el => {
            el.addEventListener('input', calculateTPD);
        });

        function calculateTPD() {
            // Inputs
            const numWashers = parseFloat(document.getElementById('numWashers').value) || 0;
            const numDryers = parseFloat(document.getElementById('numDryers').value) || 0;
            const washPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('washPrice').value) || 0;
            const dryPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('dryPrice').value) || 0;
            const cycleTime = parseFloat(document.getElementById('avgCycleTime').value) || 35;
            const hoursOpen = parseFloat(document.getElementById('operatingHours').value) || 16;
            const occupancy = parseFloat(document.getElementById('occupancyRate').value) / 100 || 0.65;
            const dailyExpenses = parseFloat(document.getElementById('dailyExpenses').value) || 0;

            // Calculations
            const maxTurnsPerMachine = (hoursOpen * 60) / cycleTime;
            const actualTPD = maxTurnsPerMachine * occupancy;
            
            const dailyWashRevenue = numWashers * actualTPD * washPrice;
            const dailyDryRevenue = numDryers * actualTPD * dryPrice;
            const totalDailyRevenue = dailyWashRevenue + dailyDryRevenue;
            const dailyProfit = totalDailyRevenue - dailyExpenses;
            
            const breakEvenTPD = dailyExpenses / ((numWashers * washPrice) + (numDryers * dryPrice));
            const annualProfit = dailyProfit * 365;
            const fiveYearProfit = annualProfit * 5;

            // Update Metrics
            document.getElementById('tpdValue').textContent = actualTPD.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('dailyRevenue').textContent = '$' + totalDailyRevenue.toFixed(0);
            document.getElementById('breakEvenTPD').textContent = breakEvenTPD.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('fiveYearProfit').textContent = '$' + fiveYearProfit.toFixed(0);

            // Update Charts
            updateTPDChart(actualTPD, breakEvenTPD);
            updateRevenueChart(dailyWashRevenue, dailyDryRevenue);
            updateProfitChart(dailyProfit);
            updateOccupancyChart(occupancy, totalDailyRevenue);
        }

        function updateTPDChart(actual, breakEven) {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('tpdChart').getContext('2d');
            if (tpdChart) tpdChart.destroy();
            tpdChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'bar',
                data: {
                    labels: ['Current TPD', 'Break-Even TPD'],
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Turns Per Day',
                        data: [actual, breakEven],
                        backgroundColor: [actual > breakEven ? '#28a745' : '#dc3545', '#ffc107'],
                        borderRadius: 8,
                        borderSkipped: false
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                    scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { display: false } } }
                }
            });
        }

        function updateRevenueChart(wash, dry) {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('revenueChart').getContext('2d');
            if (revenueChart) revenueChart.destroy();
            revenueChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'doughnut',
                data: {
                    labels: ['Wash Revenue', 'Dry Revenue'],
                    datasets: [{
                        data: [wash, dry],
                        backgroundColor: ['#007bff', '#00c6ff'],
                        borderWidth: 0,
                        borderRadius: 8
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom' } }
                }
            });
        }

        function updateProfitChart(dailyProfit) {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('profitChart').getContext('2d');
            if (profitChart) profitChart.destroy();
            profitChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: ['Year 1', 'Year 2', 'Year 3', 'Year 4', 'Year 5'],
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Cumulative Profit',
                        data: [dailyProfit*365, dailyProfit*365*2, dailyProfit*365*3, dailyProfit*365*4, dailyProfit*365*5],
                        borderColor: '#28a745',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(40,167,69,0.1)',
                        fill: true,
                        tension: 0.4,
                        pointBackgroundColor: '#28a745',
                        pointRadius: 6
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                    scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { display: false } } }
                }
            });
        }

        function updateOccupancyChart(currentOcc, currentRevenue) {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('occupancyChart').getContext('2d');
            if (occupancyChart) occupancyChart.destroy();
            
            const occLevels = [50, 60, 70, 80, 90];
            const revenues = occLevels.map(occ => {
                const factor = occ / 100 / (document.getElementById('occupancyRate').value / 100);
                return currentRevenue * factor;
            });

            occupancyChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: occLevels.map(o => o + '%'),
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Daily Revenue',
                        data: revenues,
                        borderColor: '#ffc107',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,193,7,0.1)',
                        fill: true,
                        tension: 0.4,
                        pointBackgroundColor: occLevels.indexOf(parseInt(document.getElementById('occupancyRate').value)) !== -1 ? '#dc3545' : '#ffc107',
                        pointRadius: occLevels.indexOf(parseInt(document.getElementById('occupancyRate').value)) !== -1 ? 8 : 5
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                    scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { display: false } } }
                }
            });
        }

        // PDF Export
        function exportPDF() {
            const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
            const doc = new jsPDF();
            let y = 20;

            // Header
            doc.setFontSize(22);
            doc.setTextColor(0, 123, 255);
            doc.text("WashBizHub TPD Report", 105, y, { align: "center" }); y += 15;
            doc.setFontSize(10);
            doc.setTextColor(100);
            doc.text(`Generated: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`, 105, y, { align: "center" }); y += 20;

            // Summary
            doc.setFontSize(14);
            doc.setTextColor(0);
            doc.text("TPD Summary", 20, y); y += 10;
            doc.setFontSize(11);
            [
                `True TPD: ${document.getElementById('tpdValue').textContent}`,
                `Daily Revenue: ${document.getElementById('dailyRevenue').textContent}`,
                `Break-Even TPD: ${document.getElementById('breakEvenTPD').textContent}`,
                `5-Year Profit: ${document.getElementById('fiveYearProfit').textContent}`
            ].forEach(line => { doc.text(line, 20, y); y += 7; });

            doc.save(`WashBizHub_TPD_Report_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.pdf`);
        }

        // Initialize
        window.onload = () => {
            calculateTPD();
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

        .export-btn { background: linear-gradient(135deg, #6f42c1, #8e5ce8); color: white; padding: 0.75rem 2rem; border: none; border-radius: 50px; font-size: 1rem; font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; transition: all 0.3s; box-shadow: 0 8px 25px rgba(111,66,193,0.3); }
        .export-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-3px); box-shadow: 0 12px 30px rgba(111,66,193,0.4); }

        /* Footer */

        .footer-links a { color: #ccc; text-decoration: none; transition: color 0.3s; }
        .footer-links a:hover { color: white; }

        }

    <script>
        // Chart Instances
        let tpdChart, revenueChart, profitChart, occupancyChart, priceChart, expenseChart, machineChart;

        // Real-time occupancy display
        document.getElementById('occupancyRate').addEventListener('input', function() {
            document.getElementById('occupancyValue').textContent = this.value + '%';
            calculateTPD();
        });

        // Auto-calculate on any input change
        document.querySelectorAll('input, select').forEach(el => {
            el.addEventListener('input', calculateTPD);
        });

        function calculateTPD() {
            // Inputs
            const numWashers = parseFloat(document.getElementById('numWashers').value) || 0;
            const numDryers = parseFloat(document.getElementById('numDryers').value) || 0;
            const washPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('washPrice').value) || 0;
            const dryPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('dryPrice').value) || 0;
            const cycleTime = parseFloat(document.getElementById('avgCycleTime').value) || 35;
            const hoursOpen = parseFloat(document.getElementById('operatingHours').value) || 16;
            const occupancy = parseFloat(document.getElementById('occupancyRate').value) / 100 || 0.65;
            const dailyExpenses = parseFloat(document.getElementById('dailyExpenses').value) || 0;

            // Calculations
            const maxTurnsPerMachine = (hoursOpen * 60) / cycleTime;
            const actualTPD = maxTurnsPerMachine * occupancy;
            
            const dailyWashRevenue = numWashers * actualTPD * washPrice;
            const dailyDryRevenue = numDryers * actualTPD * dryPrice;
            const totalDailyRevenue = dailyWashRevenue + dailyDryRevenue;
            const dailyProfit = totalDailyRevenue - dailyExpenses;
            
            const breakEvenTPD = dailyExpenses / ((numWashers * washPrice) + (numDryers * dryPrice));
            const annualProfit = dailyProfit * 365;
            const fiveYearProfit = annualProfit * 5;

            // Update Metrics
            document.getElementById('tpdValue').textContent = actualTPD.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('dailyRevenue').textContent = '$' + totalDailyRevenue.toFixed(0);
            document.getElementById('breakEvenTPD').textContent = breakEvenTPD.toFixed(2);
            document.getElementById('fiveYearProfit').textContent = '$' + fiveYearProfit.toFixed(0);

            // Update Charts
            updateTPDChart(actualTPD, breakEvenTPD);
            updateRevenueChart(dailyWashRevenue, dailyDryRevenue);
            updateProfitChart(dailyProfit);
            updateOccupancyChart(occupancy, totalDailyRevenue);
            updateSensitivityCharts(washPrice, dryPrice, dailyExpenses, numWashers + numDryers, totalDailyRevenue);
        }

        function updateTPDChart(actual, breakEven) {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('tpdChart').getContext('2d');
            if (tpdChart) tpdChart.destroy();
            tpdChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'bar',
                data: {
                    labels: ['Current TPD', 'Break-Even TPD'],
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Turns Per Day',
                        data: [actual, breakEven],
                        backgroundColor: [actual > breakEven ? '#28a745' : '#dc3545', '#ffc107'],
                        borderRadius: 8,
                        borderSkipped: false
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                    scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { display: false } } }
                }
            });
        }

        function updateRevenueChart(wash, dry) {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('revenueChart').getContext('2d');
            if (revenueChart) revenueChart.destroy();
            revenueChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'doughnut',
                data: {
                    labels: ['Wash Revenue', 'Dry Revenue'],
                    datasets: [{
                        data: [wash, dry],
                        backgroundColor: ['#007bff', '#00c6ff'],
                        borderWidth: 0,
                        borderRadius: 8
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    plugins: { legend: { position: 'bottom' } }
                }
            });
        }

        function updateProfitChart(dailyProfit) {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('profitChart').getContext('2d');
            if (profitChart) profitChart.destroy();
            profitChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: ['Year 1', 'Year 2', 'Year 3', 'Year 4', 'Year 5'],
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Cumulative Profit',
                        data: [dailyProfit*365, dailyProfit*365*2, dailyProfit*365*3, dailyProfit*365*4, dailyProfit*365*5],
                        borderColor: '#28a745',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(40,167,69,0.1)',
                        fill: true,
                        tension: 0.4,
                        pointBackgroundColor: '#28a745',
                        pointRadius: 6
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                    scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { display: false } } }
                }
            });
        }

        function updateOccupancyChart(currentOcc, currentRevenue) {
            const ctx = document.getElementById('occupancyChart').getContext('2d');
            if (occupancyChart) occupancyChart.destroy();
            
            const occLevels = [50, 60, 70, 80, 90];
            const revenues = occLevels.map(occ => {
                const factor = occ / 100 / currentOcc;
                return currentRevenue * factor;
            });

            occupancyChart = new Chart(ctx, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: occLevels.map(o => o + '%'),
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Daily Revenue',
                        data: revenues,
                        borderColor: '#ffc107',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,193,7,0.1)',
                        fill: true,
                        tension: 0.4,
                        pointBackgroundColor: occLevels.indexOf(Math.round(currentOcc * 100)) !== -1 ? '#dc3545' : '#ffc107',
                        pointRadius: occLevels.indexOf(Math.round(currentOcc * 100)) !== -1 ? 8 : 5
                    }]
                },
                options: {
                    responsive: true,
                    plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                    scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { display: false } } }
                }
            });
        }

        // Sensitivity Analysis Charts
        function updateSensitivityCharts(washPrice, dryPrice, dailyExpenses, totalMachines, baseRevenue) {
            // Price Sensitivity (Wash Price ±20%)
            const priceLevels = [washPrice * 0.8, washPrice * 0.9, washPrice, washPrice * 1.1, washPrice * 1.2];
            const priceRevenues = priceLevels.map(p => {
                const washRev = totalMachines * (document.getElementById('occupancyRate').value / 100) * (document.getElementById('operatingHours').value * 60 / document.getElementById('avgCycleTime').value) * p;
                const dryRev = totalMachines * (document.getElementById('occupancyRate').value / 100) * (document.getElementById('operatingHours').value * 60 / document.getElementById('avgCycleTime').value) * dryPrice;
                return (washRev + dryRev) - dailyExpenses;
            });

            const ctx1 = document.getElementById('priceChart').getContext('2d');
            if (priceChart) priceChart.destroy();
            priceChart = new Chart(ctx1, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: priceLevels.map(p => '$' + p.toFixed(2)),
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Daily Profit',
                        data: priceRevenues,
                        borderColor: '#6f42c1',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(111,66,193,0.1)',
                        fill: true,
                        tension: 0.4
                    }]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true } } }
            });

            // Expense Sensitivity (±30%)
            const expLevels = [dailyExpenses * 0.7, dailyExpenses * 0.85, dailyExpenses, dailyExpenses * 1.15, dailyExpenses * 1.3];
            const expProfits = expLevels.map(e => baseRevenue - e);

            const ctx2 = document.getElementById('expenseChart').getContext('2d');
            if (expenseChart) expenseChart.destroy();
            expenseChart = new Chart(ctx2, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: expLevels.map(e => '$' + e.toFixed(0)),
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Daily Profit',
                        data: expProfits,
                        borderColor: '#dc3545',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(220,53,69,0.1)',
                        fill: true,
                        tension: 0.4
                    }]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true } } }
            });

            // Machine Count Sensitivity (±30%)
            const machineLevels = [Math.round(totalMachines * 0.7), Math.round(totalMachines * 0.85), totalMachines, Math.round(totalMachines * 1.15), Math.round(totalMachines * 1.3)];
            const machineRevenues = machineLevels.map(m => {
                const perMachineRev = baseRevenue / totalMachines;
                return perMachineRev * m - dailyExpenses;
            });

            const ctx3 = document.getElementById('machineChart').getContext('2d');
            if (machineChart) machineChart.destroy();
            machineChart = new Chart(ctx3, {
                type: 'line',
                data: {
                    labels: machineLevels.map(m => m + ' machines'),
                    datasets: [{
                        label: 'Daily Profit',
                        data: machineRevenues,
                        borderColor: '#28a745',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(40,167,69,0.1)',
                        fill: true,
                        tension: 0.4
                    }]
                },
                options: { responsive: true, plugins: { legend: { display: false } }, scales: { y: { beginAtZero: true } } }
            });
        }

        // PDF Export
        function exportPDF() {
            const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;
            const doc = new jsPDF();
            let y = 20;

            // Header
            doc.setFontSize(22);
            doc.setTextColor(0, 123, 255);
            doc.text("WashBizHub TPD Report", 105, y, { align: "center" }); y += 15;
            doc.setFontSize(10);
            doc.setTextColor(100);
            doc.text(`Generated: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`, 105, y, { align: "center" }); y += 20;

            // Summary
            doc.setFontSize(14);
            doc.setTextColor(0);
            doc.text("TPD Summary", 20, y); y += 10;
            doc.setFontSize(11);
            [
                `True TPD: ${document.getElementById('tpdValue').textContent}`,
                `Daily Revenue: ${document.getElementById('dailyRevenue').textContent}`,
                `Break-Even TPD: ${document.getElementById('breakEvenTPD').textContent}`,
                `5-Year Profit: ${document.getElementById('fiveYearProfit').textContent}`
            ].forEach(line => { doc.text(line, 20, y); y += 7; });

            doc.save(`WashBizHub_TPD_Report_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.pdf`);
        }

        // Initialize
        window.onload = () => {
            calculateTPD();
        };
    </script>
</body>
</html>

Welcome to the WashBizHub Funding Hub—your one-stop resource for securing smart, tailored financing for your commercial laundry business. Whether you're launching a startup laundromat, upgrading equipment like Speed Queen washers or Huebsch dryers, expanding with real estate, or covering working capital for WDF/PUD services, we've got you covered. This hub breaks down every funding type with minimum requirements, an interactive comparison chart, lender recommendations, and no-credit-impact prequal links to help you decide what's best—without hard pulls.
Powered by WashBizHub's three generations of expertise, this guide is updated for 2025 rates and programs, including SBA adjustments for inflation (e.g., 7(a) max $5M at 7-10% rates). Explore options below, use our interactive matcher for personalization, or calculate payments to see ROI. Ready to apply? Jump to prequals—no credit hit!
Why Funding Matters for Laundromats in 2025
Laundromats are recession-proof cash cows (avg ROI 20-35%), but starting or scaling requires $50K-$5M. Traditional banks are slow; SBA and alternative lenders offer faster paths with lower barriers. Key trends: Green incentives for energy-efficient machines (up to $50K grants), and 100% equipment financing amid rising costs. Read our 2025 Funding Trends Report for full insights.

Every Type of Funding Offered for Laundromats
We've detailed 8 core funding types below, with pros, cons, and use cases. All are accessible via SBA or private lenders—start with our free prequal tool to match.

SBA 7(a) Loans
Versatile workhorse for startups/expansions. Covers equipment (washers/dryers), working capital, or debt refinance.
Min Requirements: 680+ credit, 10% down, 2+ years in business (or strong plan for startups), $50K min.
Pros: Low rates (7-10%), long terms (up to 25 years), up to $5M.
Cons: 90-day approval; paperwork-heavy.
Best For: Full laundromat builds or acquisitions. Learn more on SBA.gov.

SBA 504 Loans
Fixed-rate for big assets like real estate or heavy equipment.
Min Requirements: 680+ credit, 10-20% down, job creation (1 per $75K loaned), established business.
Pros: 20-25 year terms, rates ~5-7%, up to $5.5M.
Cons: Limited to fixed assets (no working capital).
Best For: Buying/renovating laundromat buildings. SBA 504 Guide.

SBA Microloans
Small, accessible for beginners.
Min Requirements: 575+ credit, business plan, no down (or minimal), via nonprofits.
Pros: Up to $50K, rates 8-13%, terms up to 6 years.
Cons: Small size; intermediary lenders only.
Best For: Startup inventory or initial equipment. Microloan Program.

Equipment Financing/Leasing
100% coverage for machines (e.g., Speed Queen, Maytag).
Min Requirements: 600+ credit, 3+ months in business, equipment as collateral.
Pros: No down payment, rates 4.99-10%, terms 36-96 months.
Cons: Higher rates for leases; tied to assets.
Best For: Upgrading washers/dryers. Equipment Loan Guide.

Term Loans (Bank/Alternative)
Lump-sum for general use.
Min Requirements: 660+ credit, 6+ months in business, $100K revenue.
Pros: Up to $2M, rates 6-12%, terms 1-5 years.
Cons: Personal guarantee; faster alternatives cost more.
Best For: Working capital or renovations. Term Loan Comparison.

Lines of Credit
Revolving funds for ongoing needs.
Min Requirements: 670+ credit, 1+ year in business, $120K revenue.
Pros: Draw as needed (up to $250K), rates 7-15%.
Cons: Variable rates; daily/weekly payments.
Best For: Seasonal cash flow. LOC for Laundromats.

Merchant Cash Advances (MCA)
Revenue-based quick cash.
Min Requirements: 500+ credit, 3+ months, $100K revenue.
Pros: Same-day funding up to $500K, no collateral.
Cons: Factor rates (1.1-1.5), daily deductions.
Best For: Emergency upgrades. MCA Guide.

Grants & Incentives
Free money for qualified projects.
Min Requirements: Varies (e.g., minority-owned, green tech); no repayment.
Pros: $5K-$100K, non-dilutive.
Cons: Competitive; application-heavy.
Best For: Eco-upgrades or startups. Grant Search.



Interactive Funding Comparison Chart
Use this chart to compare options at a glance. Hover for details; click to prequal (no credit impact!).
Grok can make mistakes. Always check original sources.
Chart Key: Scale 1-5 (higher = better). E.g., SBA 7(a) excels in amount/term but lags in speed. Learn TPD for ROI to pair with funding.

Which Funding Company is Good for What?
Here's our expert take—matched to laundromat needs, with your prequal links (soft pull only). We've prioritized lenders from your network for commissions.

For Startups ($10K-$50K, Low Credit): SBA Microloans via ROK Financial or GoKapital—quick $50K for initial machines. Prequal: ROK Soft Prequal (2 min, no hit).
For Equipment Upgrades ($50K-$1M, 600+ Credit): Equipment Financing via SouthEnd Capital or David Allen Capital—100% coverage for washers. Prequal: SouthEnd Instant Quote (no credit check).
For Expansions/Real Estate ($500K-$5M, 680+ Credit): SBA 7(a)/504 via Advanced Funds Network—long terms for buildings. Prequal: AFN Prequal Form (soft pull).
For Working Capital ($10K-$500K, Fair Credit): Term Loans/MCA via Preferred Funding Group—fast cash for WDF services. Prequal: PFG Quick Check (1 min, no impact).
For Quick Cash ($5K-$250K, Any Credit): Lines of Credit via GoKapital—draw as needed for inventory. Prequal: GoKapital Soft Inquiry.

Compare all with our matcher—or export your custom plan as PDF.

Deciding What's Best for Your Situation
No one-size-fits-all—use this framework:

Assess Needs: Startup? Microloans. Expansion? SBA 504. Quick cash? MCA.
Check Eligibility: Use prequals above (all soft/no hit).
Calculate Impact: Run TPD/ROI tools to forecast returns.
Apply Strategically: Start with SBA for low rates; fallback to alternatives for speed.

Pro Tip: Combine funding (e.g., SBA for equipment + grant for green upgrades). Book a free consult to review your plan. Questions? Contact us.

Every Type of Funding Offered for Laundromats
Here's a breakdown of 8 essential funding types, tailored for laundromats (e.g., equipment like Speed Queen washers, real estate, or WDF expansions). Each includes pros/cons and best uses, with minimum requirements compared to your affiliates.

SBA 7(a) Loans
Versatile SBA-guaranteed loans for startups/expansions (up to $5M).
Pros: Low rates (7-10%), long terms (up to 25 years), flexible use (equipment/working capital).
Cons: 90-day approval, paperwork-heavy.
Best For: Full builds or acquisitions. SBA Guide.

SBA 504 Loans
Fixed-rate for fixed assets (up to $5.5M).
Pros: 20-year terms, rates ~5-7%, job creation focus.
Cons: Limited to real estate/equipment, 10-20% down.
Best For: Buying laundromat buildings. SBA 504 Details.

SBA Microloans
Small SBA loans via nonprofits (up to $50K).
Pros: Low down (0-10%), rates 8-13%, beginner-friendly.
Cons: Small size, 6-year terms.
Best For: Initial inventory/equipment. Microloan Program.

Equipment Financing/Leasing
Asset-based loans (up to $1M+).
Pros: 0% down, rates 4.99-10%, terms 36-96 months.
Cons: Equipment as collateral.
Best For: Washers/dryers upgrades. Equipment Guide.

Term Loans (Bank/Alternative)
Lump-sum with fixed payments (up to $2M).
Pros: Rates 6-12%, terms 1-5 years.
Cons: 20% down, personal guarantee.
Best For: Working capital/renovations. Term Loan Comparison.

Lines of Credit
Revolving draw (up to $250K).
Pros: Pay interest only on used funds, rates 7-15%.
Cons: Variable rates, daily payments.
Best For: Seasonal cash flow. LOC Guide.

Merchant Cash Advances (MCA)
Revenue-based quick cash (up to $500K).
Pros: Same-day funding, no collateral.
Cons: Factor rates 1.1-1.5, daily deductions.
Best For: Emergencies. MCA Guide

'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Menu, X, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';
export default function Header() {
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [openDropdown, setOpenDropdown] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const dropdowns = {
    strategy: [
      { name: "CLEANBI Valuation", href: "/cleanbi" },
      { name: "Design Studio", href: "/design" },
      { name: "POS Dashboard", href: "/pos" },
      { name: "Calculators", href: "/calculators" },
    ],
    funding: [
      { name: "Funding Hub", href: "/funding" },
      { name: "Consultation", href: "/consult" },
    ],
    growth: [
      { name: "Marketplace", href: "/marketplace" },
      { name: "Parts & Supplies", href: "/parts" },
      { name: "Masterclass", href: "/masterclass" },
      { name: "Vault", href: "/vault" },
    ],
  };
  return (
    <header className="fixed top-0 w-full bg-navy/95 backdrop-blur-2xl z-50 border-b border-gold-400/20">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-6 py-4 flex justify-between items-center">
        {/* Logo + Tagline */}
        <div className="flex flex-col">
          <Link href="/" className="text-5xl md:text-6xl font-black bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-green-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
            WASHBIZHUB
          </Link>
          <p className="hidden lg:block text-xs md:text-sm text-yellow-400/80 tracking-widest font-medium mt-1">
            STRATEGY • FUNDING • GROWTH
          </p>
        </div>
        {/* Desktop Navigation */}
        <nav className="hidden lg:flex items-center space-x-10 text-lg font-medium">
          {Object.entries(dropdowns).map(([key, items]) => (
            <div
              key={key}
              className="relative"
              onMouseEnter={() => setOpenDropdown(key)}
              onMouseLeave={() => setOpenDropdown(null)}
            >
              <button className="flex items-center gap-2 hover:text-gold-400 transition capitalize">
                {key}
                <ChevronDown className={w-4 h-4 transition-transform ${openDropdown === key ? 'rotate-180' : ''}} />
              </button>
              <AnimatePresence>
                {openDropdown === key && (
                  <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                    className="absolute top-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-navy/98 backdrop-blur-2xl rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-gold-400/30 py-6 px-8 min-w-max"
                  >
                    {items.map((item) => (
                      <Link
                        key={item.href}
                        href={item.href}
                        className="block py-3 px-6 rounded-xl hover:bg-gold-400/10 hover:text-gold-400 transition whitespace-nowrap"
                      >
                        {item.name}
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </motion.div>
                )}
              </AnimatePresence>
            </div>
          ))}
          <Link href="/" className="hover:text-gold-400 transition">Home</Link>
          <Link href="/marketplace" className="hover:text-gold-400 transition">Marketplace</Link>
        </nav>
        {/* CTA + Mobile Menu */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
          <a
            href="tel:479-926-5273"
            className="hidden md:block bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-green-400 text-navy px-10 py-4 rounded-full font-black text-xl hover:scale-105 transition shadow-2xl"
          >
            Call Nick
          </a>
          {/* Mobile Hamburger */}
          <button
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)}
            className="lg:hidden text-4xl text-yellow-400"
          >
            {mobileOpen ? <X /> : <Menu />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Mobile Menu */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {mobileOpen && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ height: 0 }}
            animate={{ height: 'auto' }}
            exit={{ height: 0 }}
            className="lg:hidden bg-navy/98 backdrop-blur-2xl border-t border-gold-400/30"
          >
            <div className="px-8 py-10 space-y-8 text-2xl font-medium">
              <Link href="/" className="block hover:text-gold-400">Home</Link>
              <Link href="/marketplace" className="block hover:text-gold-400">Marketplace</Link>
              <Link href="/cleanbi" className="block hover:text-gold-400">CLEANBI</Link>
              <Link href="/pos" className="block hover:text-gold-400">POS</Link>
              <Link href="/design" className="block hover:text-gold-400">Design Studio</Link>
              <Link href="/vault" className="block hover:text-gold-400">Vault</Link>
              <Link href="/masterclass" className="block hover:text-gold-400">Masterclass</Link>
              <a href="tel:479-926-5273" className="block bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-green-400 text-navy py-8 rounded-full text-center font-black">
                Call Nick
              </a>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </header>
  );
}PERFECT. I SEE IT NOW — THIS IS THE VIBE.

                <ul>
                    <li><strong>Min Requirements:</strong> 680+ credit, 10% down, 2+ years in business (or strong plan), $50K min.</li>
                    <li><strong>Pros:</strong> Low rates (7-10%), long terms (up to 25 years).</li>
                    <li><strong>Cons:</strong> 90-day approval, paperwork-heavy.</li>
                    <li><strong>Best For:</strong> Full laundromat builds or acquisitions.</li>
                </ul>

                <ul>
                    <li><strong>Min Requirements:</strong> 680+ credit, 10-20% down, job creation (1 per $75K), established business.</li>
                    <li><strong>Pros:</strong> 20-year terms, rates ~5-7%.</li>

                    <li><strong>Best For:</strong> Initial inventory/equipment.</li>

                    <li><strong>Pros:</strong> 0% down, rates 4.99-10%, terms 36-96 months.</li>
                    <li><strong>Cons:</strong> Tied to assets.</li>

                    <li><strong>Cons:</strong> Factor rates 1.1-1.5, daily deductions.</li>
                    <li><strong>Best For:</strong> Emergencies.</li>

            Which Lender for What?
            
                
                    ROK Financial
                    SBA 7(a) specialist—up to $5M for startups/expansions. 680+ credit, 10% down. Rates 7-10%.
                    Prequal (No Credit Hit)
                
                
                    SouthEnd Capital
                    SBA 504 for real estate/equipment. $100K-$15M, 15% down, 680+ credit. Terms up to 20 years.
                    Prequal (No Credit Hit)
                
                
                    David Allen Capital
                    Term loans/MCA up to $2M, 500+ credit, 3 months in business. Same-day funding for working capital.
                    Prequal (No Credit Hit)
                
                
                    GoKapital
                    Lines of credit up to $50M, 660+ credit, $100K revenue. Flexible draw for seasonal needs.
                    Prequal (No Credit Hit)
                
                
                    Advanced Funds Network
                    Equipment financing up to $500K, 600+ credit, no down. 4.99% rates for machines.
                    Prequal (No Credit Hit)
                
                
                    Preferred Funding Group
                    Microloans/grants up to $50K, 575+ credit, minimal docs. For startups/green upgrades.
                    Prequal (No Credit Hit)
                
            
            All prequals are soft inquiries—no FICO impact. Track your referrals with nkreme20 for commissions. [Full Matcher](https://washbizhub.com/funding-matcher) for personalization.

GLOBAL HEADER (used on every page)
File: components/Header.tsx (or header.html if plain HTML)
tsx'use client';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Menu, X, ChevronDown } from 'lucide-react';

export default function Header() {
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [openDropdown, setOpenDropdown] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const dropdowns = {
    strategy: [
      { name: "CLEANBI Valuation", href: "/cleanbi" },
      { name: "Design Studio", href: "/design" },
      { name: "POS Dashboard", href: "/pos" },
      { name: "Calculators", href: "/calculators" },
    ],
    funding: [
      { name: "Funding Hub", href: "/funding" },
      { name: "Consultation", href: "/consult" },
    ],
    growth: [
      { name: "Marketplace", href: "/marketplace" },
      { name: "Parts & Supplies", href: "/parts" },
      { name: "Masterclass", href: "/masterclass" },
      { name: "Vault", href: "/vault" },
    ],
  };

  return (
    <header className="fixed top-0 w-full bg-navy/95 backdrop-blur-2xl z-50 border-b border-gold-400/20">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-6 py-4 flex justify-between items-center">
        <div className="flex flex-col">
          <Link href="/" className="text-5xl md:text-6xl font-black bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-green-400 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
            WASHBIZHUB
          </Link>
          <p className="hidden lg:block text-xs md:text-sm text-yellow-400/80 tracking-widest font-medium mt-1">
            STRATEGY • FUNDING • GROWTH
          </p>
        </div>

        <nav className="hidden lg:flex items-center space-x-10 text-lg font-medium">
          {Object.entries(dropdowns).map(([key, items]) => (
            <div key={key} className="relative" onMouseEnter={() => setOpenDropdown(key)} onMouseLeave={() => setOpenDropdown(null)}>
              <button className="flex items-center gap-2 hover:text-gold-400 transition capitalize">
                {key}
                <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 transition-transform ${openDropdown === key ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`} />
              </button>
              <AnimatePresence>
                {openDropdown === key && (
                  <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
                    className="absolute top-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-navy/98 backdrop-blur-2xl rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-gold-400/30 py-6 px-8 min-w-max"
                  >
                    {items.map((item) => (
                      <Link key={item.href} href={item.href} className="block py-3 px-6 rounded-xl hover:bg-gold-400/10 hover:text-gold-400 transition whitespace-nowrap">
                        {item.name}
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </motion.div>
                )}
              </AnimatePresence>
            </div>
          ))}
          <Link href="/" className="hover:text-gold-400 transition">Home</Link>
          <Link href="/marketplace" className="hover:text-gold-400 transition">Marketplace</Link>
        </nav>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
          <a href="tel:479-926-5273" className="hidden md:block bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-green-400 text-navy px-10 py-4 rounded-full font-black text-xl hover:scale-105 transition shadow-2xl">
            Call Nick
          </a>
          <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)} className="lg:hidden text-4xl text-yellow-400">
            {mobileOpen ? <X /> : <Menu />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <AnimatePresence>
        {mobileOpen && (
          <motion.div initial={{ height: 0 }} animate={{ height: 'auto' }} exit={{ height: 0 }} className="lg:hidden bg-navy/98 backdrop-blur-2xl border-t border-gold-400/30">
            <div className="px-8 py-10 space-y-8 text-2xl font-medium">
              <Link href="/" className="block hover:text-gold-400">Home</Link>
              <Link href="/marketplace" className="block hover:text-gold-400">Marketplace</Link>
              <Link href="/cleanbi" className="block hover:text-gold-400">CLEANBI</Link>
              <Link href="/pos" className="block hover:text-gold-400">POS</Link>
              <Link href="/funding" className="block hover:text-gold-400">Funding</Link>
              <Link href="/vault" className="block hover:text-gold-400">Vault</Link>
              <a href="tel:479-926-5273" className="block bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-green-400 text-navy py-8 rounded-full text-center font-black">
                Call Nick
              </a>
            </div>
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </header>
  );
}
